country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.401
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_defense_nomadic.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			is_gestalt = no is_nomadic = yes
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_defense_nomadic } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_defense_nomadic
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_defense_nomadic }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense_nomadic } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense_nomadic
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_defense_nomadic
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_defense_nomadic
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			is_gestalt = no is_nomadic = yes
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_defense_nomadic } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_defense_nomadic
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_defense_nomadic }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense_nomadic } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense_nomadic
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_defense_nomadic
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_defense_nomadic
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			is_gestalt = no is_nomadic = yes
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_defense_nomadic } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_defense_nomadic
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_defense_nomadic }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense_nomadic } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense_nomadic
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_defense_nomadic
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_defense_nomadic
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			is_gestalt = no is_nomadic = yes
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_defense_nomadic } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_defense_nomadic
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_defense_nomadic }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense_nomadic } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense_nomadic
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_defense_nomadic
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_defense_nomadic
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				is_gestalt = no is_nomadic = yes
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_defense_nomadic } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.401 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.402
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_defense.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			is_gestalt = no is_nomadic = no
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_defense } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_defense
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_defense }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_defense
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_defense
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_defense
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }

					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_beacon_of_liberty }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_beacon_of_liberty } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_beacon_of_liberty
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_beacon_of_liberty
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_beacon_of_liberty
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_beacon_of_liberty
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_beacon_of_liberty } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_beacon_of_liberty

				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1005 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1008
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_citizen_service.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_citizen_service
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_citizen_service } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_citizen_service
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_citizen_service }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_citizen_service } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_citizen_service
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_citizen_service
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_citizen_service
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }

		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_corvee_system
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_corvee_system } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1010 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1012
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_crusader_spirit.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_crusader_spirit
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_crusader_spirit } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_crusader_spirit
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_crusader_spirit }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_crusader_spirit } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_crusader_spirit
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_crusader_spirit
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_crusader_spirit
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_crusader_spirit
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_crusader_spirit } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_crusader_spirit
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_crusader_spirit }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_crusader_spirit } }

			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_feudal_realm
			}

				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1022 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1028
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_inwards_perfection.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_inwards_perfection
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_inwards_perfection } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"

				}
			}

				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_inwards_perfection

			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_inwards_perfection
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1038
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_police_state.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_police_state
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_police_state } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_police_state
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_police_state }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_police_state } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_police_state
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_police_state
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_police_state
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_police_state
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_police_state } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_police_state
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_police_state }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_police_state } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_police_state
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_police_state
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_police_state
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_police_state
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_police_state } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_police_state
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_police_state }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_police_state } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_police_state

				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_police_state
			}

				set_council_position = councilor_police_state
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_police_state
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_police_state } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_police_state
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_police_state }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_police_state } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_police_state
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_police_state
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_police_state
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_police_state
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_police_state } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1038 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_warrior_culture
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_warrior_culture } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_warrior_culture
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_warrior_culture }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_warrior_culture } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_warrior_culture
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_warrior_culture
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_warrior_culture
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_warrior_culture
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_warrior_culture } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_warrior_culture
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_warrior_culture }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_warrior_culture } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_warrior_culture
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_warrior_culture
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_warrior_culture
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_warrior_culture
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_warrior_culture } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1050 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1055
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_head_flight_operations.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_flight_schools
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_head_flight_operations } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_head_flight_operations
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_head_flight_operations }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_head_flight_operations } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_head_flight_operations
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_head_flight_operations
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_head_flight_operations
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_flight_schools
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_head_flight_operations } }

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_criminal_heritage
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_criminal_heritage } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_criminal_heritage
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_criminal_heritage }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_criminal_heritage } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_criminal_heritage
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_criminal_heritage
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_criminal_heritage
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

	}

	}

	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}

		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_naval_contractors
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_naval_contractors } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_naval_contractors
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_naval_contractors }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_naval_contractors } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_naval_contractors
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_naval_contractors
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_naval_contractors
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_naval_contractors
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_naval_contractors } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1073 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

		}

				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_trading_posts } }

				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_trading_posts
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_trading_posts
			}
		}

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				is_gestalt = yes
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_gestalt_legion } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1086 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1089
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_galactic_sovereign.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_galactic_sovereign
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_galactic_sovereign } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_galactic_sovereign
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_galactic_sovereign }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_galactic_sovereign } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_galactic_sovereign
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_galactic_sovereign
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_galactic_sovereign
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_galactic_sovereign
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_galactic_sovereign } }
		}

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_sovereign_guardianship
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sovereign_guardianship_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_sovereign_guardianship_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_sovereign_guardianship_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sovereign_guardianship_corporate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sovereign_guardianship_corporate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_sovereign_guardianship_corporate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_sovereign_guardianship_corporate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }

				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1095 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1102
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_individual_machine_warbots.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_individual_machine_warbots
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_individual_machine_warbots } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_individual_machine_warbots
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_individual_machine_warbots }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_individual_machine_warbots } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_individual_machine_warbots
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_individual_machine_warbots
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_individual_machine_warbots
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_country_flag = unlocks_warden_of_the_citadel
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_warden_of_the_citadel } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1120 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1121
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_infernal_scorcher.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_scorched_earth
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_infernal_scorcher } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_infernal_scorcher
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_infernal_scorcher }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_infernal_scorcher } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_infernal_scorcher
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_infernal_scorcher
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_infernal_scorcher
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_scorched_earth
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_infernal_scorcher } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"

			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_SH_ship
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_SH_ship }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_SH_ship } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_SH_ship
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_SH_ship
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_SH_ship
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}

		trigger = {

			has_valid_civic = civic_SH_ship
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_SH_ship } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"

			has_valid_civic = civic_SH_ship
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_SH_ship } }

		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_SH_ship
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_SH_ship } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1152 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1215
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_sh_yaosai.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_yaosai
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_yaosai } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_sh_yaosai
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_sh_yaosai }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_yaosai } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_yaosai
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_sh_yaosai
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_sh_yaosai
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_yaosai
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_yaosai } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_sh_yaosai
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_sh_yaosai }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_yaosai } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_yaosai
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_sh_yaosai
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_sh_yaosai
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_yaosai
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_yaosai } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_sh_yaosai
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_sh_yaosai }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_yaosai } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_yaosai
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_sh_yaosai
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_sh_yaosai
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_yaosai
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_yaosai } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_sh_yaosai
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_sh_yaosai }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_yaosai } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_yaosai
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_sh_yaosai
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_sh_yaosai
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_yaosai
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_yaosai } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1215 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_uf_5
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_uf_5 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_uf_5
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_uf_5 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_uf_5 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_uf_5
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_uf_5
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_uf_5
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_uf_5
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_uf_5 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_uf_5
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_uf_5 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_uf_5 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_uf_5
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_uf_5
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_uf_5
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_uf_5
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_uf_5 } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1225 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.40 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}
